-- ================= أدوات =================
local function new(class, props, parent)
    local o = Instance.new(class)
    for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do o[k] = v end
    if parent then o.Parent = parent end
    return o
end
local function corner(o, r) return new("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r or 8)}, o) end
local function stroke(o, col, th, tr)
    return new("UIStroke", {Color = col, Thickness = th or 1, Transparency = tr or 0,
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border}, o)
end

-- حذف أي نسخة قديمة
do
    local places = {}
    pcall(function() table.insert(places, gethui and gethui() or nil) end)
    pcall(function() table.insert(places, game:GetService("CoreGui")) end)
    pcall(function() table.insert(places, lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")) end)
    for _, p in ipairs(places) do
        for _, n in ipairs({"AstaTop", "AstaShadow", "AstaAuto"}) do
            local old = p and p:FindFirstChild(n)
            if old then old:Destroy() end
        end
    end
end

end

-- ================= مقدمة AS X ZY (بدون كلمة مرور) =================
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local PASS_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(0, 150, 255)   -- لون شاشة اختيار اللغة (غيّره لأي لون RGB)
local GATE = {introActive = false, pending = nil, onDone = nil}   -- رسائل السكن تنتظر لين تخلص المقدمة

local B64 = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
local B64MAP = {}
for i = 1, #B64 do B64MAP[string.sub(B64, i, i)] = i - 1 end
local function b64decode(data)
    data = string.gsub(data, "[^%w%+/]", "")
    local out, buf, bits, n = {}, 0, 0, 0
    for i = 1, #data do
        if i % 40000 == 0 then task.wait() end
        buf = buf * 64 + B64MAP[string.sub(data, i, i)]
        bits = bits + 6
        if bits >= 8 then
            bits = bits - 8
            local p2 = 2 ^ bits
            n = n + 1
            out[n] = string.char(math.floor(buf / p2) % 256)
            buf = buf % p2
        end
    end
    return table.concat(out)
end

local function loadAsset(name, override, embed, cb)
    if override == "" and embed == "" then return end      -- نسخة خفيفة: ما فيه صورة مدمجة
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, asset = pcall(function()
            if override ~= "" then
                if not string.find(override, "^https?://") then return override end
                writefile("asta_top_" .. name .. ".png", game:HttpGet(override))
                return getcustomasset("asta_top_" .. name .. ".png")
            end
            writefile("asta_top_" .. name .. ".jpg", b64decode(embed))
            return getcustomasset("asta_top_" .. name .. ".jpg")
        end)
        if ok and asset then cb(asset) end
    end)
end

-- ================= اللغة / Language =================
local LANG = "ar"
local function T(ar, en) return LANG == "ar" and ar or en end
do
    local picked = false
    local overlay = new("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.1, ZIndex = 500, Active = true}, gui)
    local box = new("Frame", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 330, 0, 230), BackgroundColor3 = C.bg, ZIndex = 501}, overlay)
    corner(box, 18); stroke(box, PASS_COLOR, 2, 0.1)
    new("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 14), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40),
        Text = "Asta Top 1👑", TextColor3 = PASS_COLOR, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack, TextSize = 30, ZIndex = 502}, box)
    new("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 56), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22),
        Text = "Select Language / اختر اللغة", TextColor3 = C.sub, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 15, ZIndex = 502}, box)
    local function langBtn(y, text, code)
        local b = new("TextButton", {Text = text, Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -120, 0, y), Size = UDim2.new(0, 240, 0, 44),
            BackgroundColor3 = C.off, TextColor3 = C.text, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 18, ZIndex = 502,
            AutoButtonColor = true}, box)
        corner(b, 12); stroke(b, PASS_COLOR, 1.5, 0.4)
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() LANG = code; picked = true end)
    end
    langBtn(96, "English", "en")
    langBtn(152, "العربية", "ar")
    while not picked and gui.Parent do task.wait(0.1) end
    overlay:Destroy()
end


do   -- ================= مقدمة AS X ZY الفخمة =================
    local INTRO_TIME   = 15      -- مدة المقدمة بالثواني (الأغنية كاملة 26 — غيّرها لو تبي)
    local INTRO_VOLUME = 1       -- صوت المقدمة
    local GOLD  = Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 40)
    local GOLD2 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 236, 160)
    local WHITE = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 250)

    local TS, Debris = game:GetService("TweenService"), game:GetService("Debris")
    local skipped = false
    local function tw(o, t, props, style, dir)
        local tween = TS:Create(o, TweenInfo.new(t, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, dir or Enum.EasingDirection.Out), props)
        tween:Play()
        return tween
    end
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local vs = (cam and cam.ViewportSize) or Vector2.new(800, 400)
    local TSIZE = math.clamp(math.floor(vs.Y * 0.2), 40, 110)

    local ov = new("Frame", {Name = "XZYIntro", Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
        BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 600, Active = true, ClipsDescendants = true}, gui)
    local function live() return ov.Parent ~= nil and not skipped end
    local function wt(t) local e = os.clock() + t; while os.clock() < e and live() do task.wait() end return live() end
    local function mk(class, props, parent) props.ZIndex = props.ZIndex or 603; props.BorderSizePixel = 0; return new(class, props, parent or ov) end

    -- ===== تحميل الصوت (والشاشة سوداء) =====
    local loadLbl = mk("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 24), Text = "• • •", TextColor3 = GOLD, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 18})
    local snd
    pcall(function()
        local clean = string.gsub(SONG_DATA.xzy_intro, "[^%w%+/=]", "")
        local native = (crypt and (crypt.base64decode or (crypt.base64 and crypt.base64.decode)))
            or (syn and syn.crypt and syn.crypt.base64 and syn.crypt.base64.decode)
            or base64_decode or (base64 and base64.decode)
        local data
        if native then
            local ok, res = pcall(native, clean)
            if ok and type(res) == "string" and string.sub(res, 1, 4) == "OggS" then data = res end
        end
        data = data or b64decode(clean)
        writefile("AstaXZY_intro.ogg", data)
        snd = Instance.new("Sound")
        snd.Name = "XZYIntroSound"; snd.SoundId = getcustomasset("AstaXZY_intro.ogg")
        snd.Volume = INTRO_VOLUME; snd.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")
    end)
    loadLbl:Destroy()

    -- ===== العناصر =====
    local glow = mk("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.45, 0),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 185, 30), ZIndex = 601})
    new("UIGradient", {Rotation = 90, Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.85), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})}, glow)
    tw(glow, 2.2, {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.8, 0)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine)
    task.delay(2.3, function() if glow.Parent then
        TS:Create(glow, TweenInfo.new(2, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true), {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.55, 0)}):Play() end end)

    local pf = mk("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 602})
    local P = {}
    for i = 1, 48 do
        local s = math.random(2, 5)
        local f = mk("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(0, s, 0, s), Rotation = 45, ZIndex = 602,
            BackgroundColor3 = (math.random() < 0.6) and GOLD or WHITE, BackgroundTransparency = math.random(30, 80) / 100}, pf)
        P[i] = {f = f, x = math.random(), y = math.random(), v = math.random(4, 14) / 100, ph = math.random() * 6}
    end
    local scan = mk("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = GOLD, BackgroundTransparency = 0.8, ZIndex = 604})

    local stage = mk("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 603})

    local crown = mk("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, -0.2, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 120, 0, TSIZE * 0.7), Text = "👑", TextSize = math.floor(TSIZE * 0.6), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextTransparency = 1}, stage)

    local function titleLabel(col, tr)
        return mk("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.42, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, TSIZE + 20), Text = "AS X ZY", TextColor3 = col, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack,
            TextSize = TSIZE, TextTransparency = tr, ZIndex = 603}, stage)
    end
    local gR = titleLabel(Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 80), 1); gR.ZIndex = 603
    local gC = titleLabel(Color3.fromRGB(0, 220, 255), 1); gC.ZIndex = 603
    local title = titleLabel(WHITE, 0); title.ZIndex = 605
    title.RichText = true
    title.Text = 'AS <font color="#FFCD28">X</font> ZY'
    new("UIGradient", {Color = ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, WHITE), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, GOLD2),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, WHITE)})}, title)
    pcall(function() title.MaxVisibleGraphemes = 0; gR.MaxVisibleGraphemes = 0; gC.MaxVisibleGraphemes = 0 end)

    local line = mk("Frame", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.545, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 2),
        BackgroundColor3 = GOLD}, stage)
    new("UIGradient", {Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})}, line)

    local sub = mk("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.62, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30), Text = "A S T A   T O P   1  👑", TextColor3 = GOLD, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
        TextSize = math.max(16, math.floor(TSIZE * 0.3)), TextTransparency = 1}, stage)
    local nm = tostring(lp.DisplayName or lp.Name)
    local tag = mk("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.70, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24), Text = T("أهلاً بك يا " .. nm .. " في القمة", "WELCOME TO THE TOP, " .. string.upper(nm)),
        TextColor3 = WHITE, Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium, TextSize = 15, TextTransparency = 1}, stage)

    -- شريط التحميل
    local ldLbl = mk("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.84, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 18), Text = "LOADING ASTA HUB  0%", TextColor3 = C.sub, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 12,
        TextTransparency = 1}, stage)
    local ldBg = mk("Frame", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.84, 4), Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 4),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 42), BackgroundTransparency = 1}, stage)
    local ldFill = mk("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = GOLD, BackgroundTransparency = 1}, ldBg)
    new("UIGradient", {Color = ColorSequence.new(GOLD, GOLD2)}, ldFill)

    -- أشرطة سينمائية
    local topBar = mk("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.12, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, -0.12, 0), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), ZIndex = 610})
    mk("Frame", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = GOLD, ZIndex = 611}, topBar)
    local botBar = mk("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.12, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), ZIndex = 610})
    mk("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = GOLD, ZIndex = 611}, botBar)
    mk("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0), Text = "© AS X ZY",
        TextColor3 = C.sub, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 13, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ZIndex = 612}, botBar)
    tw(topBar, 0.8, {Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart)
    tw(botBar, 0.8, {Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.88, 0)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart)

    -- زر التخطي
    local skip = mk("TextButton", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1), Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -8), Size = UDim2.new(0, 112, 0, 36),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 24), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = T("تخطي ⏭", "SKIP ⏭"), TextColor3 = GOLD,
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack, TextSize = 16, TextTransparency = 1, AutoButtonColor = true, ZIndex = 620})
    corner(skip, 18)
    local skipStroke = stroke(skip, GOLD, 1.5, 1)
    skip.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() skipped = true end)
    task.delay(0.4, function() if skip.Parent then
        tw(skip, 0.5, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.15, TextTransparency = 0}); tw(skipStroke, 0.5, {Transparency = 0.2}) end end)

    -- ===== مؤثرات =====
    local function shake(power, dur)
        task.spawn(function()
            local t0 = os.clock()
            while os.clock() - t0 < dur and ov.Parent do
                local k = 1 - (os.clock() - t0) / dur
                stage.Position = UDim2.new(0, math.random(-power, power) * k, 0, math.random(-power, power) * k)
                task.wait()
            end
            stage.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        end)
    end
    local function flash(a)
        local f = mk("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BackgroundTransparency = a or 0.3, ZIndex = 615})
        tw(f, 0.5, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}); Debris:AddItem(f, 0.6)
    end
    local function ring()
        local r = mk("Frame", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.42, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 606}, stage)
        corner(r, 1000)
        local st = stroke(r, GOLD, 3, 0)
        tw(r, 0.9, {Size = UDim2.new(0, vs.X * 0.8, 0, vs.X * 0.8)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart)
        tw(st, 0.9, {Transparency = 1})
        Debris:AddItem(r, 1)
    end
    local function glitch(dur)
        task.spawn(function()
            local t0 = os.clock()
            gR.TextTransparency, gC.TextTransparency = 0.3, 0.3
            while os.clock() - t0 < dur and ov.Parent do
                gR.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, math.random(-9, 9), 0.42, math.random(-3, 3))
                gC.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, math.random(-9, 9), 0.42, math.random(-3, 3))
                title.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, math.random(-3, 3), 0.42, 0)
                task.wait(0.03)
            end
            gR.TextTransparency, gC.TextTransparency = 1, 1
            gR.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.42, 0); gC.Position = gR.Position; title.Position = gR.Position
        end)
    end

    -- جسيمات + خط المسح + شريط التحميل
    local t0 = os.clock()
    local last = os.clock()
    local hb = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        local now = os.clock(); local dt = now - last; last = now
        for _, p in ipairs(P) do
            p.y = p.y - p.v * dt
            if p.y < -0.05 then p.y = 1.05; p.x = math.random() end
            p.f.Position = UDim2.new(p.x + math.sin(now * 1.2 + p.ph) * 0.012, 0, p.y, 0)
        end
        scan.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, (now * 0.3) % 1, 0)
        local a = math.clamp((now - t0) / INTRO_TIME, 0, 1)
        ldFill.Size = UDim2.new(a, 0, 1, 0)
        ldLbl.Text = "LOADING ASTA HUB  " .. math.floor(a * 100) .. "%"
    end)

    -- ===== السيناريو =====
    if snd then pcall(function() snd:Play() end) end
    t0 = os.clock()
    task.spawn(function()
        if not wt(0.5) then return end
        tw(line, 1, {Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0, 2)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart)
        crown.TextTransparency = 0
        tw(crown, 0.9, {Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.2, 0)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Bounce)
        tw(ldLbl, 0.6, {TextTransparency = 0.2}); tw(ldBg, 0.6, {BackgroundTransparency = 0}); tw(ldFill, 0.6, {BackgroundTransparency = 0})
        if not wt(0.9) then return end
        for i = 1, 7 do                                  -- ظهور الحروف حرف حرف
            pcall(function() title.MaxVisibleGraphemes = i; gR.MaxVisibleGraphemes = i; gC.MaxVisibleGraphemes = i end)
            shake(5, 0.12); flash(0.85)
            if not wt(0.16) then return end
        end
        pcall(function() title.MaxVisibleGraphemes = -1; gR.MaxVisibleGraphemes = -1; gC.MaxVisibleGraphemes = -1 end)
        flash(0.15); shake(16, 0.6); ring(); task.delay(0.2, ring); glitch(0.6)
        tw(sub, 0.8, {TextTransparency = 0}); tw(tag, 1.2, {TextTransparency = 0.15})
        pcall(function()
            local g = title:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
            TS:Create(g, TweenInfo.new(1.6, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true), {Offset = Vector2.new(0.6, 0)}):Play()
        end)
        while live() do                                  -- جلتشات عشوائية طول المقدمة
            if not wt(math.random(15, 30) / 10) then return end
            glitch(0.25); shake(6, 0.2)
        end
    end)

    while live() and os.clock() - t0 < INTRO_TIME do task.wait(0.05) end

    -- ===== الخروج =====
    hb:Disconnect()
    local out = new("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 700}, gui)
    local fast = skipped
    tw(out, fast and 0.15 or 0.4, {BackgroundTransparency = 0}).Completed:Wait()
    ov:Destroy()
    tw(out, 0.7, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}); Debris:AddItem(out, 0.9)
    if snd then
        local s = snd
        pcall(function() tw(s, fast and 0.4 or 1.2, {Volume = 0}) end)
        task.delay(1.4, function() pcall(function() s:Destroy() end) end)
    end
end

-- ================= الشخصية والخصم =================
local function getChar(p)
    local c = p and p.Character
    if not c then return nil end
    local h = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local r = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if h and r and h.Health > 0 then return c, h, r end
    return nil
end

local function nearestTarget(myRoot)
    local best, bd = nil, math.huge
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= lp then
            local _, _, r = getChar(p)
            if r then
                local d = (r.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                if d < bd then best, bd = r, d end
            end
        end
    end
    return best
end

-- كشف القنبلة: الاوتو ما يروح للخصم إلا لما تكون القنبلة عندك
local function nameLooksBomb(n)
    n = string.lower(n)
    return string.find(n, "bomb", 1, true) ~= nil or string.find(n, "tnt", 1, true) ~= nil
        or string.find(n, "dynamite", 1, true) ~= nil
end
local function hasBomb(p)
    local c = p.Character
    if not c then return false end
    for _, item in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
        if item:IsA("Tool") then return true end
    end
    for _, n in ipairs({"HasBomb", "Bomb", "IsBomb", "BombHolder", "Holding", "IsIt", "It", "hasBomb"}) do
        if c:GetAttribute(n) == true or p:GetAttribute(n) == true then return true end
    end
    for _, d in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
        if nameLooksBomb(d.Name) then
            if d:IsA("BoolValue") then
                if d.Value then return true end
            elseif d:IsA("BasePart") or d:IsA("Model") or d:IsA("Tool") then
                return true
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end

-- إذا تمشي بنفسك (عصا الجوال / كيبورد) الاوتو ما يتدخل
local controls = nil
task.spawn(function()
    pcall(function()
        local ps = lp:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts", 5)
        local pm = ps and ps:WaitForChild("PlayerModule", 5)
        if pm then controls = require(pm):GetControls() end
    end)
end)
local function userIsMoving()
    if controls then
        local ok, v = pcall(function() return controls:GetMoveVector() end)
        if ok and v then return v.Magnitude > 0.1 end
    end
    local k = Enum.KeyCode
    return UserInputService:IsKeyDown(k.W) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(k.A)
        or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(k.S) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(k.D)
end

-- ================= السكن =================
local InsertService = game:GetService("InsertService")
local toast = function(_) end          -- تتعرف في الواجهة (رسالة صغيرة تحت الزر)
local quietToast = false               -- يمنع رسالة "تم تطبيق السكن" وقت الترقيع الصامت
local skinActive, skinTexture = false, nil
local skinGuis = {}                    -- كل شي ضفناه (يتحذف عند الإرجاع)
local saved = nil                      -- شكلك الأصلي

local function skinConfigured()
    return SKIN.username ~= "" or SKIN.userId > 0 or SKIN.shirt > 0 or SKIN.pants > 0 or SKIN.graphicTShirt > 0
        or SKIN.face > 0 or #SKIN.hair > 0 or #SKIN.hats > 0
end

local assetCache = {}
local function loadAssetObj(id)
    if assetCache[id] then return assetCache[id]:Clone() end
    local obj = nil
    local ok, objs = pcall(function() return game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id)) end)
    if ok and objs and objs[1] then obj = objs[1] end
    if not obj then
        local ok2, model = pcall(function() return InsertService:LoadAsset(id) end)
        if ok2 and model then
            local kids = model:GetChildren()
            if kids[1] then obj = kids[1] end
        end
    end
    if obj then
        local okc, cl = pcall(function() return obj:Clone() end)
        if okc and cl then assetCache[id] = cl end
    end
    return obj
end

-- نحفظ شكلك الأصلي وننزع الملابس والاكسسوارات (نرجعها بعدين)
local hideConn
local getDesc            -- تتعرف تحت (تجميد السكنات)
local origDesc, fakeLegParts = nil, {}
local function snapshotAndStrip(char)
    saved = {removed = {}, colors = nil, face = nil}
    for _, d in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if (d:IsA("Accessory") and not nameLooksBomb(d.Name)) or d:IsA("Shirt") or d:IsA("Pants") or d:IsA("ShirtGraphic") then
            table.insert(saved.removed, d)
            d.Parent = nil
        end
    end
    local bc = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyColors")
    if bc then saved.colors = bc:Clone() end
    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    local face = head and head:FindFirstChildOfClass("Decal")
    if face then saved.face = face.Texture end
end

local function restoreVisual(char)
    if hideConn then hideConn:Disconnect(); hideConn = nil end
    for _, fp in ipairs(fakeLegParts) do pcall(function() fp:Destroy() end) end
    fakeLegParts = {}
    if origDesc then
        local c = lp.Character
        local hm = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hm then pcall(function() hm:ApplyDescription(origDesc) end) end
        origDesc = nil
    end
    for _, g in ipairs(skinGuis) do pcall(function() g:Destroy() end) end
    skinGuis = {}
    if not saved then return end
    char = char or (lp.Character)
    if char then
        for _, d in ipairs(saved.removed) do
            pcall(function() d.Parent = char end)
        end
        local bc = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyColors")
        if bc and saved.colors then
            for _, prop in ipairs({"HeadColor3", "TorsoColor3", "LeftArmColor3", "RightArmColor3", "LeftLegColor3", "RightLegColor3"}) do
                local orig = saved.colors[prop]
                bc[prop] = Color3.new(1, 0, 1)
                bc[prop] = orig
            end
        end
        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        local face = head and head:FindFirstChildOfClass("Decal")
        if face and saved.face then face.Texture = saved.face end
    end
    if saved.transp then
        for inst, val in pairs(saved.transp) do
            pcall(function() inst.Transparency = val end)
        end
    end
    if saved.headMesh then
        local hm = saved.headMesh
        pcall(function()
            hm.mesh.MeshType = hm.MeshType; hm.mesh.MeshId = hm.MeshId; hm.mesh.TextureId = hm.TextureId
            hm.mesh.Scale = hm.Scale; hm.mesh.Offset = hm.Offset
        end)
    end
    saved = nil
end

-- يركّب الاكسسوار على الشخصية (AddAccessory، وإذا ما التحم نلحمه بنفسنا)
local function isWelded(handle)
    for _, d in ipairs(handle:GetChildren()) do
        if d:IsA("Weld") or d:IsA("WeldConstraint") or d:IsA("Motor6D") then return true end
    end
    return false
end
local function attachAccessory(char, h, acc)
    pcall(function() h:AddAccessory(acc) end)
    if acc.Parent ~= char then acc.Parent = char end
    local handle = acc:FindFirstChild("Handle")
    if not handle or not handle:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
    if isWelded(handle) then return true end
    handle.CanCollide = false
    handle.Massless = true
    local att = handle:FindFirstChildOfClass("Attachment")
    local target = nil
    if att then
        for _, part in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                local a = part:FindFirstChild(att.Name)
                if a and a:IsA("Attachment") then target = a break end
            end
        end
    end
    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if target then
        handle.CFrame = target.WorldCFrame * att.CFrame:Inverse()
        local w = Instance.new("Weld")
        w.Part0 = target.Parent; w.Part1 = handle
        w.C0 = target.CFrame; w.C1 = att.CFrame
        w.Parent = handle
        return true
    elseif head then
        handle.CFrame = head.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, head.Size.Y / 2, 0)
        local w = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
        w.Part0 = head; w.Part1 = handle; w.Parent = handle
        return true
    end
    return false
end

local function applyKorblox(char)
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then return false end
    local ok = pcall(function()
        local d = hum:GetAppliedDescription()
        origDesc = d:Clone()
        d.RightLeg = KORBLOX_ID
        hum:ApplyDescription(d)
    end)
    return ok
end
-- رجل كوربلوكس: مسمار صغير رفيع أخضر مزرق فقط (بدون أي شي أسود)
local LEG_X, LEG_TOP = 0.5, -1.0      -- مكانه: يمين/يسار ، وارتفاع البداية
local LEG_W = 0.14                    -- العرض
local LEG_TOP_LEN, LEG_TIP_LEN = 0.4, 0.6   -- طول الجزء العلوي ، طول الطرف المدبب
local function buildFakeLeg(char)
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return end
    local darkTeal = Color3.fromRGB(8, 100, 100)
    local teal     = Color3.fromRGB(40, 190, 190)
    local function add(inst, off, size, col)
        inst.Size = size; inst.Color = col
        inst.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
        inst.CanCollide = false; inst.CanTouch = false; inst.CanQuery = false
        inst.Massless = true; inst.Anchored = false
        inst.CFrame = hrp.CFrame * off
        local w = Instance.new("WeldConstraint"); w.Part0 = hrp; w.Part1 = inst; w.Parent = inst
        inst.Parent = char
        table.insert(fakeLegParts, inst)
    end
    local zHalf = 0.1
    add(Instance.new("Part"), CFrame.new(LEG_X, LEG_TOP - LEG_TOP_LEN / 2, 0),
        Vector3.new(LEG_W, LEG_TOP_LEN, zHalf * 2), darkTeal)
    local y2 = LEG_TOP - LEG_TOP_LEN - LEG_TIP_LEN / 2
    add(Instance.new("WedgePart"), CFrame.new(LEG_X, y2, -zHalf / 2) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi),
        Vector3.new(LEG_W, LEG_TIP_LEN, zHalf), teal)
    add(Instance.new("WedgePart"), CFrame.new(LEG_X, y2, zHalf / 2) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.pi, math.pi),
        Vector3.new(LEG_W, LEG_TIP_LEN, zHalf), teal)
end

-- ينسخ رجل اليمين من أفاتار الحساب (e4cyz) بنفس شكلها بالضبط
local dummyCache, modelCache = {}, {}
local function buildUserLeg(char, desc, key)
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not (hum and desc) then return false end
    local cacheKey = key and (key .. tostring(hum.RigType))
    local okM, dummy = true, nil
    if cacheKey and dummyCache[cacheKey] then
        dummy = dummyCache[cacheKey]:Clone()
    else
        okM, dummy = pcall(function()
            return Players:CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription(desc, hum.RigType)
        end)
        if okM and dummy and cacheKey then dummyCache[cacheKey] = dummy:Clone() end
    end
    if not (okM and dummy) then return false end
    if hum.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
        -- في R6 شكل الرجل الخاصة يجي من CharacterMesh مو من الجزء نفسه
        for _, d in ipairs(dummy:GetChildren()) do
            if d:IsA("CharacterMesh") and d.BodyPart == Enum.BodyPart.RightLeg then
                local cm = d:Clone()
                cm.Parent = char
                table.insert(fakeLegParts, cm)
            end
        end
        pcall(function() dummy:Destroy() end)
        return true, true   -- native: نخلي الرجل الأصلية ظاهرة
    end
    local names = {"RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot"}
    local made = 0
    for _, n in ipairs(names) do
        local src, dst = dummy:FindFirstChild(n), char:FindFirstChild(n)
        if src and dst then
            local c = src:Clone()
            for _, d in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
                if d:IsA("Motor6D") or d:IsA("Weld") or d:IsA("WeldConstraint") then d:Destroy() end
            end
            c.Name = "KX_" .. n
            c.Anchored = false; c.CanCollide = false; c.CanTouch = false; c.CanQuery = false
            c.Massless = true; c.Transparency = 0
            c.CFrame = dst.CFrame
            local w = Instance.new("WeldConstraint"); w.Part0 = dst; w.Part1 = c; w.Parent = c
            c.Parent = char
            table.insert(fakeLegParts, c)
            made = made + 1
        end
    end
    pcall(function() dummy:Destroy() end)
    return made > 0
end

-- تطبيق أفاتار (من حساب أو من أرقام) يدويًا على شخصيتك: ملابس + اكسسوارات + ألوان + وجه
local function applySpec(char, h, spec)
    local korbloxOk, nativeLeg = false, false
    local useFake = false
    if KORBLOX and spec.desc then
        korbloxOk, nativeLeg = buildUserLeg(char, spec.desc, spec.key)
        if not korbloxOk then useFake = true end
    elseif KORBLOX then
        useFake = true
    end
    snapshotAndStrip(char)
    if useFake then buildFakeLeg(char) end
    local count = 0
    for _, key in ipairs({"Shirt", "Pants", "GraphicTShirt"}) do
        local id = spec[key]
        if id and id > 0 then
            local obj = loadAssetObj(id)
            if obj then obj.Parent = char; table.insert(skinGuis, obj); count = count + 1 end
        end
    end
    local accOk, accTotal = 0, #(spec.accs or {})
    for _, id in ipairs(spec.accs or {}) do
        local obj = loadAssetObj(id)
        if obj and obj:IsA("Accessory") then
            if attachAccessory(char, h, obj) then accOk = accOk + 1 end
            table.insert(skinGuis, obj); count = count + 1
        end
    end
    local bc = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyColors")
    if bc and spec.colors then
        for k, v in pairs(spec.colors) do pcall(function() bc[k] = v end) end
    end
    -- إخفاء الراس والرجل (يتثبت كل فريم)
    saved.transp = saved.transp or {}
    local legSets = {
        Right = {"Right Leg", "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot"},
        Left  = {"Left Leg", "LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot"},
    }
    local hideLeg = HIDE_LEG
    if nativeLeg then hideLeg = "" elseif korbloxOk or useFake then hideLeg = "Right" end
    if hideConn then hideConn:Disconnect() end
    hideConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        local list = {}
        if HEADLESS then
            local hd = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if hd then
                table.insert(list, hd)
                for _, d in ipairs(hd:GetChildren()) do
                    if d:IsA("Decal") then table.insert(list, d) end
                end
            end
        end
        local names = {}
        for _, n in ipairs(legSets[hideLeg] or {}) do names[n] = true end
        if next(names) then
            for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("BasePart") and names[d.Name] then table.insert(list, d) end
            end
        end
        for _, inst in ipairs(list) do
            if saved and saved.transp[inst] == nil then saved.transp[inst] = inst.Transparency end
            inst.Transparency = 1
            if inst:IsA("BasePart") then inst.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1 end
        end
    end)

    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if head and spec.Face and spec.Face > 0 and not HEADLESS then
        local f = loadAssetObj(spec.Face)
        if f and f:IsA("Decal") then
            local old = head:FindFirstChildOfClass("Decal")
            if old then old.Parent = nil; table.insert(saved.removed, old) end
            f.Name = "face"; f.Parent = head
            table.insert(skinGuis, f); count = count + 1
        end
    end
    return count, "اكسسوارات " .. accOk .. "/" .. accTotal
end

-- السكن الافتراضي: يلوّن الجسم ويحط قميص الجمجمة على الصدر
local function applyDefaultSkin(char)
    local white = Color3.fromRGB(235, 238, 240)
    local black = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 20)
    local function paint(name, col)
        local p = char:FindFirstChild(name)
        if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then p.Color = col end
    end
    for _, n in ipairs({"Torso", "Left Leg", "Right Leg", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso",
        "LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot", "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot"}) do
        paint(n, black)
    end
    for _, n in ipairs({"Left Arm", "Right Arm", "LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand",
        "RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm", "RightHand"}) do
        paint(n, white)
    end
    -- شعر أسود شائك + تاج (يتبني من قطع ويتلحم بالراس)
    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if head then
        local hs = head.Size
        local hairCol = Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 15)
        local function piece(size, cf, col)
            local p = Instance.new("Part")
            p.Size = size
            p.Color = col or hairCol
            p.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
            p.CanCollide = false; p.CanTouch = false; p.CanQuery = false
            p.Massless = true; p.CastShadow = false
            p.CFrame = head.CFrame * cf
            p.Parent = char
            local w = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
            w.Part0 = head; w.Part1 = p; w.Parent = p
            table.insert(skinGuis, p)
        end
        local sx, sy, sz = hs.X, hs.Y, hs.Z
        piece(Vector3.new(sx * 1.1, sy * 0.4, sz * 1.1), CFrame.new(0, sy * 0.58, 0))                 -- قمة الشعر
        piece(Vector3.new(sx * 0.12, sy * 0.75, sz * 1.05), CFrame.new(sx * 0.53, sy * 0.15, 0))      -- جنب يمين
        piece(Vector3.new(sx * 0.12, sy * 0.75, sz * 1.05), CFrame.new(-sx * 0.53, sy * 0.15, 0))     -- جنب يسار
        piece(Vector3.new(sx * 1.08, sy * 0.85, sz * 0.14), CFrame.new(0, sy * 0.12, sz * 0.52))      -- ورا
        for i = -2, 2 do                                                                              -- غرة الوجه
            piece(Vector3.new(sx * 0.2, sy * (0.45 + 0.1 * math.abs(i)), sz * 0.12),
                CFrame.new(i * sx * 0.2, sy * 0.4, -sz * 0.52) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(i * 9)))
        end
        for i = 0, 6 do                                                                               -- شوك الشعر
            local ang = (i / 7) * math.pi * 2
            piece(Vector3.new(sx * 0.2, sy * 0.7, sz * 0.2),
                CFrame.new(math.cos(ang) * sx * 0.32, sy * 0.95, math.sin(ang) * sz * 0.32)
                * CFrame.Angles(math.sin(ang) * 0.45, 0, -math.cos(ang) * 0.45))
        end
        for i = 0, 7 do                                                                               -- التاج
            local ang = (i / 8) * math.pi * 2
            piece(Vector3.new(sx * 0.12, sy * 0.38, sz * 0.12),
                CFrame.new(math.cos(ang) * sx * 0.5, sy * 0.88, math.sin(ang) * sz * 0.5),
                Color3.fromRGB(35, 38, 48))
        end
        if FACE_ID > 0 then
            local face = head:FindFirstChildOfClass("Decal")
            if face then face.Texture = "rbxassetid://" .. FACE_ID end
        end
    end

    local torso = char:FindFirstChild("Torso") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
    if torso and skinTexture then
        local sg = Instance.new("SurfaceGui")
        sg.Face = Enum.NormalId.Front
        sg.SizingMode = Enum.SurfaceGuiSizingMode.PixelsPerStud
        sg.PixelsPerStud = 60
        sg.LightInfluence = 0
        sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
        sg.Adornee = torso
        sg.Parent = torso
        local img = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        img.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        img.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        img.Image = skinTexture
        if torso.Name == "UpperTorso" then            -- R15: الصدر أقصر، نقص الصورة
            img.ImageRectOffset = Vector2.new(0, 0)
            img.ImageRectSize = Vector2.new(256, 140)
        end
        img.Parent = sg
        table.insert(skinGuis, sg)
    end
end

local function applySkin()
    local c, h = getChar(lp)
    if not c then return false end
    restoreVisual(c)

    if skinConfigured() then
        local spec, err = nil, nil
        if SKIN.username ~= "" or SKIN.userId > 0 then
            local uid = SKIN.userId
            if SKIN.username ~= "" then
                local ok, res = pcall(function() return Players:GetUserIdFromNameAsync(SKIN.username) end)
                if ok then uid = res else err = "اليوزر غلط أو ما انجاب" end
            end
            if not err then
                local ok, desc = pcall(getDesc, (SKIN.username ~= "" and SKIN.username or tostring(uid)), uid)
                if ok and desc then
                    local accs = {}
                    local okA, list = pcall(function() return desc:GetAccessories(true) end)
                    if okA and list then for _, info in ipairs(list) do table.insert(accs, info.AssetId) end end
                    spec = {
                        Shirt = desc.Shirt, Pants = desc.Pants, GraphicTShirt = desc.GraphicTShirt, Face = desc.Face,
                        accs = accs, desc = desc, key = tostring(uid),
                        colors = {HeadColor3 = desc.HeadColor, TorsoColor3 = desc.TorsoColor,
                            LeftArmColor3 = desc.LeftArmColor, RightArmColor3 = desc.RightArmColor,
                            LeftLegColor3 = desc.LeftLegColor, RightLegColor3 = desc.RightLegColor},
                    }
                else
                    err = "ما قدر يجيب الأفاتار"
                end
            end
        else
            local accs = {}
            for _, id in ipairs(SKIN.hair) do table.insert(accs, id) end
            for _, id in ipairs(SKIN.hats) do table.insert(accs, id) end
            spec = {Shirt = SKIN.shirt, Pants = SKIN.pants, GraphicTShirt = SKIN.graphicTShirt, Face = SKIN.face, accs = accs}
        end

        local count, info = 0, ""
        if spec then count, info = applySpec(c, h, spec) end
        if count > 0 then
            -- (انشالت رسالة تم تطبيق السكن)
            return true
        end
        restoreVisual(c)
        toast("فشل السكن: " .. (err or "ما انحملت الأغراض") .. (SKIN_DEFAULT and " (السكن الافتراضي)" or ""))
    end

    if SKIN_DEFAULT then
        snapshotAndStrip(c)
        pcall(applyDefaultSkin, c)
        return true
    end
    return false
end

local function restoreSkin()
    skinActive = false
    restoreVisual()
end

-- ================= قائمة السكنات (نسخ أفاتار حساب بالضبط) =================
local SKINS = {
    {label = "FF_071", username = "FF_071", exact = true},      -- أول سكن (مجمّد: يظل نفس الشكل حتى لو غيّر أفاتاره)
    {label = "TLLWP", username = "TLLWP", exact = true},        -- ثاني سكن (مجمّد)
    {label = "ASOOXLI", username = "ASOOXLI", exact = true},
    {label = "UB1ER", username = "UB1ER", exact = true},

local FANCY_MIN_ACC = 4      -- أقل عدد اكسسوارات عشان السكن يُعتبر فخم (Headless/Korblox يعتبر فخم لحاله)
local LUX_OK = {}             -- الحسابات الفخمة اللي اشتغلت (نستخدمها كقاعدة لسكنات الرجل المقطوعة)

-- يلبسك نفس أفاتار الحساب بالضبط: ينسخ الملابس والاكسسوارات والألوان والوجه من أفاتار الحساب
local uidCache = {}
local function userIdOf(name)
    if uidCache[name] then return uidCache[name] end
    local ok, uid = pcall(function() return Players:GetUserIdFromNameAsync(name) end)
    if ok and uid then uidCache[name] = uid return uid end
    return nil
end

-- ينسخ أجزاء الجسم الخاصة (رأس Headless، رجل Korblox، ...) من أفاتار الحساب بنفس شكلها
local function copyBodyParts(char, h, model, opts)
    opts = opts or {}
    saved = saved or {}
    saved.transp = saved.transp or {}
    local hideList = {}
    local function hideOrig(p)
        saved.transp[p] = saved.transp[p] or p.Transparency
        table.insert(hideList, p)
        for _, d in ipairs(p:GetChildren()) do
            if d:IsA("Decal") then
                saved.transp[d] = saved.transp[d] or d.Transparency
                table.insert(hideList, d)
            end
        end
    end
    if h.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
        for _, d in ipairs(model:GetChildren()) do
            if d:IsA("CharacterMesh") then
                local cm = d:Clone(); cm.Parent = char
                table.insert(fakeLegParts, cm)
            end
        end
    end
    for _, src in ipairs(model:GetChildren()) do
        local dst = char:FindFirstChild(src.Name)
        local isHead = false
        local diffMesh = dst and src:IsA("MeshPart") and dst:IsA("MeshPart") and src.MeshId ~= dst.MeshId
        if dst and src ~= dst and (isHead or diffMesh) and src.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
            local c = src:Clone()
            for _, d in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
                if d:IsA("Motor6D") or d:IsA("Weld") or d:IsA("WeldConstraint") then d:Destroy() end
            end
            c.Name = "KX_" .. src.Name
            c.Anchored = false; c.CanCollide = false; c.CanTouch = false; c.CanQuery = false
            c.Massless = true; c.Transparency = 0
            c.CFrame = dst.CFrame
            local w = Instance.new("WeldConstraint"); w.Part0 = dst; w.Part1 = c; w.Parent = c
            c.Parent = char
            table.insert(fakeLegParts, c)
            hideOrig(dst)
        end
    end
    -- الوجه: نخلي راسك يصير نفس راس الحساب (الميش + الخامة + الوجه المرسوم)
    local hd = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    local mh = model:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if hd and mh and not opts.headless then
        -- 1) ميش/خامة الراس (فيها غالبًا وجهك القديم)
        local om, nm = hd:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpecialMesh"), mh:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpecialMesh")
        if om and nm then
            saved.headMesh = {mesh = om, MeshType = om.MeshType, MeshId = om.MeshId, TextureId = om.TextureId,
                Scale = om.Scale, Offset = om.Offset}
            pcall(function()
                om.MeshType = nm.MeshType; om.MeshId = nm.MeshId; om.TextureId = nm.TextureId
                om.Scale = nm.Scale; om.Offset = nm.Offset
            end)
        end
        -- 2) وجهك المرسوم ينخفي، ووجه الحساب ينركّب
        for _, d in ipairs(hd:GetChildren()) do
            if d:IsA("Decal") and d.Name ~= "KX_face" then
                saved.transp[d] = saved.transp[d] or d.Transparency
                table.insert(hideList, d)
            end
        end
        if not opts.noFace then
            for _, d in ipairs(mh:GetChildren()) do
                if d:IsA("Decal") then
                    local c = d:Clone(); c.Name = "KX_face"; c.Transparency = 0; c.Parent = hd
                    table.insert(fakeLegParts, c)
                end
            end
        end
    elseif hd and opts.headless then
        for _, d in ipairs(hd:GetChildren()) do
            if d:IsA("Decal") then
                saved.transp[d] = saved.transp[d] or d.Transparency
                table.insert(hideList, d)
            end
        end
    end
    local kh = char:FindFirstChild("KX_Head")
    if kh and (opts.noFace or opts.headless) then
        for _, d in ipairs(kh:GetChildren()) do
            if d:IsA("Decal") then
                saved.transp[d] = saved.transp[d] or d.Transparency
                table.insert(hideList, d)
            end
        end
    end
    if opts.headless then          -- بدون راس (Headless)
        for _, pt in ipairs({hd, kh}) do
            if pt then
                saved.transp[pt] = saved.transp[pt] or pt.Transparency
                table.insert(hideList, pt)
            end
        end
    end
    if #hideList > 0 then
        if hideConn then hideConn:Disconnect() end
        hideConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            for _, p in ipairs(hideList) do
                if p.Parent then p.Transparency = 1 end
            end
        end)
    end
end

local function copyFromModel(char, h, model, opts)
    snapshotAndStrip(char)                               -- يحفظ شكلك الأصلي وينزعه
    local count = 0
    for _, d in ipairs(model:GetChildren()) do
        if d:IsA("Accessory") then
            local acc = d:Clone()
            local handle = acc:FindFirstChild("Handle")
            if handle then                               -- نشيل لحام الموديل ونلحمه على شخصيتك
                for _, w in ipairs(handle:GetChildren()) do
                    if w:IsA("Weld") or w:IsA("WeldConstraint") or w:IsA("Motor6D") then w:Destroy() end
                end
            end
            attachAccessory(char, h, acc)
            table.insert(skinGuis, acc); count = count + 1
        elseif d:IsA("Shirt") or d:IsA("Pants") or d:IsA("ShirtGraphic") then
            local o = d:Clone(); o.Parent = char
            table.insert(skinGuis, o); count = count + 1
        end
    end
    local mbc, bc = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyColors"), char:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyColors")
    if mbc and bc then
        for _, prop in ipairs({"HeadColor3", "TorsoColor3", "LeftArmColor3", "RightArmColor3", "LeftLegColor3", "RightLegColor3"}) do
            pcall(function() bc[prop] = mbc[prop] end)
        end
    end
    local mh, hd = model:FindFirstChild("Head"), char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if mh and hd then
        local mf, cf = mh:FindFirstChildOfClass("Decal"), hd:FindFirstChildOfClass("Decal")
        if mf and cf then pcall(function() cf.Texture = mf.Texture end) end
    end
    pcall(copyBodyParts, char, h, model, opts)
    return count
end

-- تجميد السكنات: أول مرة ينحفظ شكل الحساب، وبعدها ما يتغير حتى لو صاحب الحساب غيّر أفاتاره
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local FREEZE_SKINS = true
local SKINS_FILE = "AstaGG_skins.json"      -- لإعادة الجلب من الحساب: احذف هالملف
local frozen = {}
if FREEZE_SKINS then
    pcall(function()
        if isfile and isfile(SKINS_FILE) then frozen = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(SKINS_FILE)) end
    end)
end
local function saveFrozen()
    pcall(function() if writefile then writefile(SKINS_FILE, HttpService:JSONEncode(frozen)) end end)
end
local DESC_PROPS = {"Shirt", "Pants", "GraphicTShirt", "Face", "Head", "Torso", "LeftArm", "RightArm", "LeftLeg", "RightLeg",
    "HeightScale", "WidthScale", "HeadScale", "DepthScale", "BodyTypeScale", "ProportionScale"}
local COLOR_PROPS = {"HeadColor", "TorsoColor", "LeftArmColor", "RightArmColor", "LeftLegColor", "RightLegColor"}
local function freezeDesc(d)
    local r = {p = {}, c = {}, a = {}}
    for _, k in ipairs(DESC_PROPS) do pcall(function() r.p[k] = d[k] end) end
    for _, k in ipairs(COLOR_PROPS) do pcall(function() local c = d[k]; r.c[k] = {c.R, c.G, c.B} end) end
    local ok, list = pcall(function() return d:GetAccessories(true) end)
    if ok and list then
        for _, i in ipairs(list) do
            table.insert(r.a, {AssetId = i.AssetId, Type = i.AccessoryType.Name, Layered = i.IsLayered,
                Order = i.Order, Puffiness = i.Puffiness})
        end
    end
    return r
end
local function thawDesc(r)
    local d = Instance.new("HumanoidDescription")
    for k, v in pairs(r.p or {}) do pcall(function() d[k] = v end) end
    for k, v in pairs(r.c or {}) do pcall(function() d[k] = Color3.new(v[1], v[2], v[3]) end) end
    local list = {}
    for _, i in ipairs(r.a or {}) do
        table.insert(list, {AssetId = i.AssetId, AccessoryType = Enum.AccessoryType[i.Type],
            IsLayered = i.Layered, Order = i.Order, Puffiness = i.Puffiness})
    end
    pcall(function() d:SetAccessories(list, true) end)
    return d
end
function getDesc(name, uid)
    if FREEZE_SKINS and frozen[name] then return thawDesc(frozen[name]) end
    local d = Players:GetHumanoidDescriptionFromUserId(uid)
    if FREEZE_SKINS then frozen[name] = freezeDesc(d); saveFrozen() end
    return d
end

task.spawn(function()      -- الفحص بالخلفية عشان الواجهة تفتح على طول
-- ===== فحص السكنات الفخمة: يظهر بس اللي يشتغل + شكله فخم (الفاشل يتحفظ ولا ينفحص مرة ثانية) =====
do
    frozen._bad = frozen._bad or {}
    local HEADLESS_HEAD, KORBLOX_LEG = 134082579, 139607718
    local results, pending = {}, #LUX_SKINS
    local function isFancy(d)
        local ok, list = pcall(function() return d:GetAccessories(true) end)
        local n = (ok and list) and #list or 0
        local head, leg = 0, 0
        pcall(function() head = d.Head; leg = d.RightLeg end)
        return n >= FANCY_MIN_ACC or head == HEADLESS_HEAD or leg == KORBLOX_LEG
    end
    for idx, name in ipairs(LUX_SKINS) do
        if frozen._bad[name] then
            pending = pending - 1
        elseif frozen[name] then
            local okT, dT = pcall(thawDesc, frozen[name])     -- انحفظ قبل: نفحصه بدون نت
            if okT and dT and isFancy(dT) then results[idx] = true else frozen._bad[name] = true end
            pending = pending - 1
        else
            task.spawn(function()
                task.wait(idx * 0.08)                -- نوزع الطلبات عشان ما ننحظر
                local okAll = pcall(function()
                    local uid = userIdOf(name)
                    assert(uid, "no uid")
                    local d
                    for _ = 1, 2 do                  -- محاولتين
                        local okD, res = pcall(getDesc, name, uid)
                        if okD and res then d = res break end
                        task.wait(0.5)
                    end
                    assert(d, "no desc")
                    assert(isFancy(d), "not fancy")
                    results[idx] = true
                end)
                if not okAll then
                    frozen[name] = nil
                    frozen._bad[name] = true
                end
                pending = pending - 1
            end)
        end
    end
    local t0 = os.clock()
    while pending > 0 and os.clock() - t0 < 25 do task.wait(0.1) end
    saveFrozen()
    local added = 0
    for idx, name in ipairs(LUX_SKINS) do
        if results[idx] and added < MAX_LUX then
            table.insert(SKINS, {label = "👑 " .. name, username = name, exact = true})
            table.insert(LUX_OK, name)
            added = added + 1
        end
    end
end


-- ===== سكنات فخمة بالرجل المقطوعة (Korblox) فقط، خفيفة (اكسسوارات قليلة) =====
local KORBLOX_EXTRA = {
    -- (اختياري) زيد يوزرات حسابات لابسة رجل كوربلوكس، وبيتفحص تلقائي
}
local LIGHT_MAX_ACC = 8          -- أقصى عدد اكسسوارات عشان السكن يُعتبر خفيف
local MAX_KORBLOX = 12           -- أقصى عدد سكنات رجل مقطوعة تظهر
do
    frozen._bad2 = frozen._bad2 or {}
    local names, seen = {}, {}
    for _, n in ipairs(KORBLOX_EXTRA) do if not seen[n] then seen[n] = true table.insert(names, n) end end
    for _, n in ipairs(LUX_SKINS) do if not seen[n] then seen[n] = true table.insert(names, n) end end
    local found, pending = {}, #names
    local function check(d)
        local leg = 0
        pcall(function() leg = d.RightLeg end)
        if leg ~= 139607718 then return false end
        local ok, list = pcall(function() return d:GetAccessories(true) end)
        local n = (ok and list) and #list or 0
        return n <= LIGHT_MAX_ACC
    end
    for idx, name in ipairs(names) do
        if frozen._bad2[name] then
            pending = pending - 1
        elseif frozen[name] then
            local okT, dT = pcall(thawDesc, frozen[name])
            if okT and dT and check(dT) then found[idx] = true else frozen._bad2[name] = true end
            pending = pending - 1
        else
            task.spawn(function()
                task.wait(idx * 0.08)
                local okAll = pcall(function()
                    local uid = userIdOf(name)
                    assert(uid, "no uid")
                    local d = getDesc(name, uid)
                    assert(d and check(d), "no korblox")
                    found[idx] = true
                end)
                if not okAll then frozen._bad2[name] = true end
                pending = pending - 1
            end)
        end
    end
    local t0 = os.clock()
    while pending > 0 and os.clock() - t0 < 25 do task.wait(0.1) end
    saveFrozen()
    local pos, added = 13, 0
    for idx, name in ipairs(names) do
        if found[idx] and added < MAX_KORBLOX then
            for i = #SKINS, 1, -1 do        -- نشيل النسخة المكررة من القائمة الفخمة
                if SKINS[i].username == name then table.remove(SKINS, i) end
            end
            table.insert(SKINS, pos, {label = "🦿 " .. name, username = name, exact = true})
            pos = pos + 1; added = added + 1
        end
    end
    KX_END = pos
end


-- ===== 35 سكن رجل مقطوعة (كوربلوكس): بدون أي تكرار، وكل واحد حساب جديد غير الموجودين بالقائمة =====
-- نفس أفاتار الحساب بس الرجل اليمين تتبدل بكوربلوكس. السكربت يفحص كل يوزر ويشيل اللي ما يشتغل أو أفاتاره فاضي.
-- تبي سكنات معينة؟ زيد اليوزرات في K_POOL (يتفحصون تلقائي).
local K_POOL = {
    "Builderman", "Shedletsky", "Telamon", "ReeseMcBlox", "Sharkbyte", "Quenty", "Crazyblox", "Coeptus",
    "Seranok", "Loleris", "Hexxed", "KreekCraft", "Tofuu", "Denis", "Bloxxer", "Sketch", "Alexnewtron",
    "Cindering", "Moolah", "Ruiner", "Jacobtheman", "Panzer", "Bighead", "Jaredvan", "Beeism", "Merlin",
    "Zeuxcg", "Bobbyjoe", "Isaac", "Quasar", "Pooky", "Rocky", "Spud", "Dollars", "Obliterator", "Atlas",
    "Ghoul", "Havoc", "Zephyr", "Raven", "Vortex", "Phantom", "Nova", "Cipher", "Blaze", "Frost", "Shadow",
    "Onyx", "Tempest", "Viper", "Reaper", "Specter", "Rogue", "Titan", "Ember", "Glitch", "Hunter",
}
do
    local FORCED_COUNT = 35
    frozen._bad3 = frozen._bad3 or {}
    local present = {}                       -- كل يوزر موجود بالقائمة (عشان ما نكرره)
    for _, sk in ipairs(SKINS) do
        if sk.username then present[string.lower(sk.username)] = true end
    end
    local pool, seenP = {}, {}
    local function addC(n)
        local k = string.lower(n)
        if not present[k] and not seenP[k] and not frozen._bad3[n] then
            seenP[k] = true; table.insert(pool, n)
        end
    end
    for _, n in ipairs(K_POOL) do addC(n) end
    for _, n in ipairs(LUX_SKINS) do addC(n) end      -- احتياط: الفخمة اللي ما انعرضت
    local function good(d)                            -- أفاتار فيه شي (مو افتراضي فاضي)
        local n = 0
        pcall(function() n = #d:GetAccessories(true) end)
        local shirt = 0
        pcall(function() shirt = d.Shirt end)
        return n >= 2 or shirt ~= 0
    end
    local found, pending = {}, #pool
    for idx, name in ipairs(pool) do
        if frozen[name] then
            local okT, dT = pcall(thawDesc, frozen[name])
            if okT and dT and good(dT) then found[idx] = true else frozen._bad3[name] = true end
            pending = pending - 1
        else
            task.spawn(function()
                task.wait(idx * 0.08)
                local okAll = pcall(function()
                    local uid = userIdOf(name)
                    assert(uid, "no uid")
                    local d = getDesc(name, uid)
                    assert(d and good(d), "empty avatar")
                    found[idx] = true
                end)
                if not okAll then frozen[name] = nil; frozen._bad3[name] = true end
                pending = pending - 1
            end)
        end
    end
    local t0 = os.clock()
    while pending > 0 and os.clock() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.1) end
    saveFrozen()
    local at, added = KX_END, 0
    for idx, name in ipairs(pool) do
        if found[idx] and added < FORCED_COUNT then
            table.insert(SKINS, at, {label = "🦿 " .. name, username = name, exact = true, forceKorblox = true})
            at = at + 1; added = added + 1
        end
    end
end

    pcall(buildSkinGallery)
end)

-- فحص: يطلع على الشاشة تركيب راس الحساب (صوّرها وأرسلها). اطفيه بـ DEBUG_SKIN = false
local DEBUG_SKIN = false
local function debugDump(model, char)
    local lines = {}
    local function num(s) return tostring(s):match("%d+$") or "-" end
    local function describe(inst)
        local s = inst.ClassName .. ":" .. inst.Name
        if inst:IsA("MeshPart") then s = s .. " mesh=" .. num(inst.MeshId) .. " tex=" .. num(inst.TextureID) end
        if inst:IsA("Decal") then s = s .. " tex=" .. num(inst.Texture) end
        if inst:IsA("BasePart") then s = s .. string.format(" size=%.1f,%.1f,%.1f", inst.Size.X, inst.Size.Y, inst.Size.Z) end
        return s
    end
    local mh = model:FindFirstChild("Head")
    table.insert(lines, "== راس الحساب ==")
    if mh then
        table.insert(lines, describe(mh))
        for _, d in ipairs(mh:GetChildren()) do table.insert(lines, "  - " .. describe(d)) end
    else
        table.insert(lines, "ما فيه راس")
    end
    table.insert(lines, "== اكسسوارات الحساب ==")
    for _, a in ipairs(model:GetChildren()) do
        if a:IsA("Accessory") then
            local at = ""
            pcall(function() at = a.AccessoryType.Name end)
            table.insert(lines, "  " .. a.Name .. " [" .. at .. "]")
        end
    end
    local ch = char and char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    table.insert(lines, "== راسك ==")
    if ch then
        table.insert(lines, describe(ch))
        for _, d in ipairs(ch:GetChildren()) do table.insert(lines, "  - " .. describe(d)) end
    end
    local lbl = new("TextLabel", {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0.25,
        Position = UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 0.03, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0.9, 0, 0.6, 0), TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
        Font = Enum.Font.Code, TextSize = 13, TextWrapped = true, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top, Text = table.concat(lines, "\n"), ZIndex = 300}, gui)
    task.delay(40, function() pcall(function() lbl:Destroy() end) end)
end

local function applyUserSkin(username, opts)
    local c0, h0 = getChar(lp)
    if not c0 then return false, "الشخصية مو جاهزة" end
    local ok, desc
    if opts and opts.build then
        ok, desc = pcall(function()                         -- سكن مبني (لون + رجل مقطوعة)
            local d = Instance.new("HumanoidDescription")
            local col = opts.color or Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 28)
            for _, k in ipairs({"HeadColor", "TorsoColor", "LeftArmColor", "RightArmColor", "LeftLegColor", "RightLegColor"}) do
                pcall(function() d[k] = col end)
            end
            return d
        end)
        username = "build_" .. tostring(opts.label)
    else
        local uid = userIdOf(username)
        if not uid then return false, "اليوزر ما انجاب" end
        ok, desc = pcall(getDesc, username, uid)
    end
    if not ok or not desc then return false, "ما قدر يجيب الأفاتار" end
    if opts and opts.forceKorblox then
        pcall(function() desc.RightLeg = KORBLOX_ID end)
        username = username .. "_K"
    end
    -- نجهز موديل الأفاتار قبل ما نرجّع شكلك (عشان ما يبين شكلك القديم)
    local okM, model = false, nil
    local mkey = username .. tostring(h0.RigType)
    if modelCache[mkey] then
        okM, model = true, modelCache[mkey]:Clone()
    else
        okM, model = pcall(function() return Players:CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription(desc, h0.RigType) end)
        if okM and model then modelCache[mkey] = model:Clone() end
    end
    restoreVisual(c0)                                    -- يرجّع شكلك الأصلي أول
    local c, h = getChar(lp)
    if not c then return false, "الشخصية مو جاهزة" end

    -- الطريقة 1: ننسخ الأغراض من موديل الأفاتار
    if okM and model then
        if DEBUG_SKIN then pcall(debugDump, model, c) end
        local okC = pcall(copyFromModel, c, h, model, opts)
        pcall(function() model:Destroy() end)
        if okC then return true end
        restoreVisual(c)
    end

    -- الطريقة 2: ApplyDescription
    local okO, cur = pcall(function() return h:GetAppliedDescription() end)
    if okO and cur then origDesc = cur end
    local okA = pcall(function() h:ApplyDescription(desc) end)
    if not okA then origDesc = nil return false, "فشل التطبيق" end
    for _, ch in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do          -- نعتبرها من السكن (عشان التثبيت ما يظنها غريبة)
        if ch:IsA("Accessory") or ch:IsA("Shirt") or ch:IsA("Pants") or ch:IsA("ShirtGraphic") then
            table.insert(skinGuis, ch)
        end
    end
    return true
end

local function applyCurrent()
    local sk = SKINS[currentSkin]
    if sk.exact then
        HEADLESS, KORBLOX, HIDE_LEG = false, false, ""        -- نسخة أصلية بالضبط
    else
        HEADLESS, KORBLOX, HIDE_LEG = D_HEADLESS, D_KORBLOX, D_HIDE_LEG
    end
    if sk.username or sk.build then
        local ok, err = applyUserSkin(sk.username or "", sk)
        if ok then
            -- (انشالت رسالة تم تطبيق السكن)
            return true
        end
        toast("فشل السكن: " .. tostring(err))
        return false
    end
    return applySkin() and true or false
end

-- ===== تثبيت السكن: يرجع يتطبق لحاله كل جولة جديدة (فوز / خسارة) أو لو اللعبة غيّرت شكلك =====
local applying, forceApply = false, false
local lastSkinChar = nil
local charSeen = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
local toasted = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})

local function resetForNewChar()
    saved = nil
    origDesc = nil
    restoreVisual()                 -- saved = nil: ينظف الاتصالات والأجزاء القديمة بس
    skinGuis = {}
    fakeLegParts = {}
end

local function skinIntact(c)
    if lastSkinChar ~= c then return false end
    local ours = {}
    for _, o in ipairs(skinGuis) do ours[o] = true end
    for _, ch in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
        if (ch:IsA("Accessory") and not nameLooksBomb(ch.Name)) or ch:IsA("Shirt") or ch:IsA("Pants") or ch:IsA("ShirtGraphic") then
            if not ours[ch] then return false end   -- اللعبة رجعت لبسك الافتراضي
        end
    end
    for _, o in ipairs(skinGuis) do
        if not o:IsDescendantOf(c) then return false end
    end
    for _, o in ipairs(fakeLegParts) do
        if not o:IsDescendantOf(c) then return false end
    end
    return true
end

local applyStartedAt = 0
local function doApply(silent)
    if closed then return false end
    if applying then
        if os.clock() - applyStartedAt < 8 then return false end
        applying = false                      -- التطبيق علق: نفك القفل ونعيد
    end
    applying = true
    applyStartedAt = os.clock()
    local c0 = lp.Character
    local ok, res = pcall(function()
        if lastSkinChar ~= c0 then resetForNewChar() end
        quietToast = silent and true or false
        return applyCurrent()
    end)
    quietToast = false
    applying = false
    if ok and res and lp.Character == c0 then
        lastSkinChar = c0
        return true
    end
    if not ok and lp.Character == c0 then toast("خطأ السكن: " .. tostring(res)) end
    return false
end

local function selectSkin(i)
    if closed then return end
    currentSkin = i
    skinActive = true
    forceApply = true
    refreshSkinButtons()
end

loadAsset("skin", SKIN_OVERRIDE, SKIN_EMBED, function(asset)
    skinTexture = asset
end)

-- أي شخصية جديدة (جولة جديدة / فوز / خسارة / رجوع للوبي) أو اللعبة خلصت تلبيس شكلك: نطبق السكن من جديد
table.insert(conns, lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    if closed then return end
    resetForNewChar()
    lastSkinChar = nil
end))
table.insert(conns, lp.CharacterAppearanceLoaded:Connect(function()
    if closed then return end
    lastSkinChar = nil          -- اللعبة لبّستك شكلك الافتراضي: نطبق فوقه
end))

-- المثبّت: ما يوقف ولا يستسلم أبدًا. يفحص كل 0.1 ثانية، وأي شي يخرب السكن يرجعه
task.spawn(function()
    local stamps = {}
    local fails, failChar = 0, nil
    local nextTry = 0
    while not closed do
        task.wait(0.1)
        pcall(function()
            if not skinActive or closed then return end
            local c = getChar(lp)
            if not c then return end
            if not c:FindFirstChild("Head") then return end
            if not (c:FindFirstChild("Torso") or c:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")) then return end
            if charSeen[c] == nil then charSeen[c] = os.clock() end
            local force = forceApply
            if not force then
                if os.clock() - charSeen[c] < 0.1 then return end
                if os.clock() < nextTry then return end
                if applying and os.clock() - applyStartedAt < 8 then return end
                if skinIntact(c) then fails = 0; return end
            end
            if failChar ~= c then failChar = c; fails = 0 end
            -- لو اللعبة تخرب السكن بشكل مستمر نهدّي الوتيرة (بس ما نوقف)
            local now, n = os.clock(), 0
            for i = #stamps, 1, -1 do
                if now - stamps[i] > 10 then table.remove(stamps, i) else n = n + 1 end
            end
            table.insert(stamps, now)
            nextTry = now + (n >= 8 and 2 or 0.15) + math.min(fails, 5) * 0.4
            forceApply = false
            local ok = doApply(toasted[c] and not force)
            if ok then fails = 0; toasted[c] = true else fails = fails + 1 end
        end)
    end
end)


-- ================= منطق Assist =================
local function isTeammate(p)
    local myTeam = lp.Team
    if myTeam and p.Team == myTeam then return true end
    if lp.Neutral == false and p.Neutral == false and p.TeamColor == lp.TeamColor then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

-- اللي برا القيم (لوبي / متفرج / ما دخل المباراة): الاسيست يتجاهلهم ويروح بس على خصم داخل القيم
local OUT_WORDS = {"lobby", "spectat", "waiting", "queue", "dead", "eliminat", "out", "idle", "none", "لوبي", "متفرج", "انتظار"}
local OUT_ATTRS = {"InGame", "InMatch", "InRound", "InArena", "Playing", "IsPlaying", "Alive", "InGameplay", "InLobbyGame"}
local OUT_ATTRS_TRUE = {"InLobby", "Spectating", "IsSpectating", "Spectator", "Eliminated", "Dead", "IsDead", "AFK", "Waiting"}
local function nameIsOut(n)
    n = string.lower(tostring(n or ""))
    for _, w in ipairs(OUT_WORDS) do
        if string.find(n, w, 1, true) then return true end
    end
    return false
end
local function isInMatch(p)
    local ok, res = pcall(function()
        -- 1) الفريق: اسم لوبي/متفرج، أو بدون فريق وأنا عندي فريق (يعني ما دخل القيم)
        if p.Team and nameIsOut(p.Team.Name) then return false end
        if lp.Team and not lp.Neutral and (p.Team == nil or p.Neutral) then return false end
        -- 2) خصائص تدل انه برا القيم
        for _, holder in ipairs({p, p.Character}) do
            if holder then
                for _, a in ipairs(OUT_ATTRS) do
                    if holder:GetAttribute(a) == false then return false end
                end
                for _, a in ipairs(OUT_ATTRS_TRUE) do
                    if holder:GetAttribute(a) == true then return false end
                end
                for _, a in ipairs(OUT_ATTRS) do
                    local v = holder:FindFirstChild(a)
                    if v and v:IsA("BoolValue") and v.Value == false then return false end
                end
                for _, a in ipairs(OUT_ATTRS_TRUE) do
                    local v = holder:FindFirstChild(a)
                    if v and v:IsA("BoolValue") and v.Value == true then return false end
                end
            end
        end
        return true
    end)
    return (not ok) or res
end

-- ===== داخل الساحة؟ (من الفيديو: المتفرجين واقفين على المنصة الرمادية، واللاعبين على أرضية الساحة البرتقالية) =====
-- 1) اللي واقف على نفس نوع الأرضية اللي أنا واقف عليها = داخل الساحة
-- 2) اللي اسمه يطلع في سطر البومبة (اللي فيه اسمين) = داخل القيم حتى لو أرضيته مختلفة
local FLOOR_COLOR_TOL = 0.14       -- فرق اللون المسموح بين أرضيتي وأرضيته
local floorParams = RaycastParams.new()
floorParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
floorParams.RespectCanCollide = true
local floorCache = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})      -- [player] = {inst=, t=}
local myFloor = {inst = nil, t = 0}

local function floorUnder(char)
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return nil end
    local ex = {char}
    if lp.Character and lp.Character ~= char then table.insert(ex, lp.Character) end
    floorParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = ex
    local r = workspace:Raycast(root.Position, Vector3.new(0, -25, 0), floorParams)
    return r and r.Instance or nil
end
local function sameFloor(a, b)
    if a == b then return true end
    if a.Material ~= b.Material then return false end
    local ca, cb = a.Color, b.Color
    return math.abs(ca.R - cb.R) < FLOOR_COLOR_TOL and math.abs(ca.G - cb.G) < FLOOR_COLOR_TOL
        and math.abs(ca.B - cb.B) < FLOOR_COLOR_TOL
end

-- أسماء اللاعبين اللي في سطر البومبة (يتحدث كل نص ثانية، ويظل 4 ثواني بعد ما يختفي)
local feedSet, feedAt, feedScanAt = {}, 0, 0
local function scanFeed()
    local now = os.clock()
    if now - feedScanAt < 0.5 then return end
    feedScanAt = now
    local pg = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    if not pg then return end
    local names = {}
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= lp then
            names[string.lower(p.Name)] = p
            names[string.lower(p.DisplayName)] = p
        end
    end
    local found, count = {}, 0
    pcall(function()
        for _, d in ipairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("TextLabel") and d.Visible and d.AbsoluteSize.X > 0 and not d:IsDescendantOf(gui)
                and not d:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("BillboardGui") then
                local t = string.lower((d.Text or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", ""))
                local pl = names[t]
                if pl and not found[pl] then found[pl] = true; count = count + 1 end
            end
        end
    end)
    -- لو طلع عدد كبير يعني قائمة لاعبين (مو سطر البومبة): نتجاهله
    if count >= 1 and count <= 6 then
        feedSet = found; feedAt = now
    elseif now - feedAt > 4 then
        feedSet = {}
    end
end

local function inArena(p)
    scanFeed()
    if feedSet[p] and os.clock() - feedAt < 4 then return true end
    local ch = p.Character
    if not ch then return false end
    local now = os.clock()
    -- أرضيتي (إذا أنا بالهوا نستخدم آخر أرضية)
    local f = floorUnder(lp.Character)
    if f then myFloor.inst = f; myFloor.t = now end
    local mine = myFloor.inst
    if not mine or not mine.Parent then return true end        -- ما عرفنا أرضيتي: ما نمنع أحد
    -- أرضيته (مخزّنة 0.2 ثانية)
    local c = floorCache[p]
    if not c or now - c.t > 0.2 then
        local inst = floorUnder(ch)
        c = {inst = inst or (c and c.inst), t = now}
        floorCache[p] = c
    end
    if not c.inst or not c.inst.Parent then return true end     -- بالهوا ولا عندنا سابق: ما نمنع
    return sameFloor(mine, c.inst)
end

-- اللاعبين اللون الاخضر (فريقك): الاسيست يتجاهلهم تمامًا ولا يروح عليهم
local function isGreenColor(c)
    return c and c.G > 0.45 and c.G > c.R * 1.35 and c.G > c.B * 1.35
end
local function colorsOf(inst)
    local out = {}
    if inst:IsA("Highlight") then
        table.insert(out, inst.FillColor); table.insert(out, inst.OutlineColor)
    elseif inst:IsA("SelectionBox") or inst:IsA("SelectionSphere") then
        table.insert(out, inst.Color3)
    elseif inst:IsA("TextLabel") or inst:IsA("TextButton") then
        table.insert(out, inst.TextColor3)
        if inst.BackgroundTransparency < 0.9 then table.insert(out, inst.BackgroundColor3) end
    elseif inst:IsA("ImageLabel") then
        table.insert(out, inst.ImageColor3)
    elseif inst:IsA("Frame") and inst.BackgroundTransparency < 0.9 then
        table.insert(out, inst.BackgroundColor3)
    elseif inst:IsA("UIStroke") then
        table.insert(out, inst.Color)
    end
    return out
end
local function hasGreenMark(root)
    for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
        for _, c in ipairs(colorsOf(d)) do
            if isGreenColor(c) then return true end
        end
    end
    return false
end
local greenCache, greenAt = {}, 0
local function refreshGreen()
    local now = os.clock()
    if now - greenAt < 0.6 then return end
    greenAt = now
    greenCache = {}
    local pg = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= lp then
            local green = false
            pcall(function()
                if p.Team and lp.Team and p.Team == lp.Team then green = true end
                if p.Team and isGreenColor(p.TeamColor.Color) then green = true end       -- لون الفريق اخضر
                local ch = p.Character
                if not green and ch then
                    for _, d in ipairs(ch:GetDescendants()) do
                        if d:IsA("Highlight") or d:IsA("SelectionBox") or d:IsA("BillboardGui") or d:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
                            if d:IsA("Highlight") or d:IsA("SelectionBox") then
                                for _, c in ipairs(colorsOf(d)) do if isGreenColor(c) then green = true end end
                            else
                                if hasGreenMark(d) then green = true end
                            end
                        end
                        if green then break end
                    end
                    -- علامات الاخضر اللي موضوعة بعيد عن الشخصية (Adornee)
                    if not green then
                        for _, root in ipairs({workspace, pg}) do
                            if root and not green then
                                for _, d in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
                                    if d:IsA("Highlight") or d:IsA("SelectionBox") or d:IsA("BillboardGui") then
                                        local ad = d.Adornee
                                        if ad and (ad == ch or ad:IsDescendantOf(ch)) then
                                            if d:IsA("BillboardGui") then
                                                if hasGreenMark(d) then green = true end
                                            else
                                                for _, c in ipairs(colorsOf(d)) do if isGreenColor(c) then green = true end end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            greenCache[p] = green
        end
    end
end


-- ================= دعس مستقل (WS Spam - MoveTo) =================
-- نفس طريقة الدعس في Zyqz: لا نرسل W/S للكيبورد، بل نستخدم Humanoid:MoveTo
-- حتى لا يعلق مؤشر الحركة أو تتأخر الشخصية بعد انتهاء الدعس.
local STOMP_BURST_DURATION = 3
local STOMP_MOVE_DISTANCE = 2.5
local STOMP_MOVE_INTERVAL = 0.07
local stompBurstRunning = false
local stompBurstToken = 0

local function startStompBurst()
    if closed or stompBurstRunning then return end

    stompBurstToken = stompBurstToken + 1
    local myToken = stompBurstToken
    stompBurstRunning = true

    task.spawn(function()
        local started = os.clock()
        local endT = started + STOMP_BURST_DURATION

        while not closed
            and myToken == stompBurstToken
            and stompBurstRunning
            and os.clock() < endT do

            local char = lp.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            if hum and hrp and hum.Health > 0 then
                -- خطوة للأمام
                local fwd = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
                hum:MoveTo(hrp.Position + fwd * STOMP_MOVE_DISTANCE)
                task.wait(STOMP_MOVE_INTERVAL)

                -- خطوة للخلف
                local char2 = lp.Character
                local hum2 = char2 and char2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local hrp2 = char2 and char2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                if hum2 and hrp2 and hum2.Health > 0
                    and not closed
                    and myToken == stompBurstToken then
                    local fwd2 = hrp2.CFrame.LookVector
                    hum2:MoveTo(hrp2.Position - fwd2 * STOMP_MOVE_DISTANCE)
                end

                task.wait(STOMP_MOVE_INTERVAL)
            else
                task.wait(STOMP_MOVE_INTERVAL)
            end
        end

        -- إيقاف الدعس فقط؛ لا نغير WalkSpeed ولا Anchored ولا PlatformStand
        -- ولا نرسل أي مفاتيح للكيبورد، لذلك التحكم الطبيعي يبقى مباشر.
        stompBurstRunning = false

        pcall(function()
            if stompToggleObj then stompToggleObj.render() end
        end)
    end)
end

-- ================= منطق Assist + تدعيس تلقائي (Auto Click) =================
local assistPower, assistRange, stopDist = 50, 30, ASSIST_STOP_DIST      -- POWER % / RANGE / المسافة اللي يوقف عندها
local PULL_MAX_SPEED = 0                                                -- سرعة السحب عند 100% باور (بلوك/ثانية)
local autoClick, clickCPS, CLICK_RANGE = false, 8, 15   -- نظام التدعيس القديم مخفي؛ زر STOMP 3s مستقل عن هذا النظام.
local targetLabel, songSound = nil, nil
local lastLbl, lastLblAt, lastClick = "", 0, 0
local function setTargetText(t)
    if targetLabel and t ~= lastLbl and os.clock() - lastLblAt > 0.15 then
        lastLbl, lastLblAt = t, os.clock()
        targetLabel.Text = t
    end
end

-- ===== تدعيس الموتى: أول ما لاعب يموت تروح فوقه وتنط عليه وتضغط الأداة (يشتغل مع زر التدعيس التلقائي) =====
-- ===== ضغط قوي (بدون متغيرات محلية جديدة عشان حد الـ 200 local) =====
local STOMP = {busy = false, list = {}, alive = {}, range = 90, life = 5}
STOMP.click = function(tool)
    pcall(function() tool:Activate() end)
    local done = false
    if mouse1click then done = pcall(mouse1click) end
    if not done then
        pcall(function()
            local vim = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
            local c = workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
            vim:SendMouseButtonEvent(c.X / 2, c.Y / 2, 0, true, game, 0)
            vim:SendMouseButtonEvent(c.X / 2, c.Y / 2, 0, false, game, 0)
        end)
    end
end
STOMP.getTool = function(ch)
    local t = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    if t then return t end
    local hum = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local bp = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
    local bt = bp and bp:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    if bt and hum then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bt) end) return bt end
    return nil
end
do
    -- يراقب كل لاعب كل 0.05 ثانية: يحفظ آخر مكان وهو حي، ولما يموت (صحة 0 / اختفى جسمه / تغيّر جسمه) يسجله
    local acc, lastJump = 0, 0
    table.insert(conns, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        if closed then return end
        acc = acc + dt
        if acc < 0.05 then return end
        acc = 0
        local now = os.clock()
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= lp then
                local ch = p.Character
