            ["FFlagAddHapticsToggle"] = "False",
            ["FFlagChatTranslationEnableSystemMessage"] = "False",
            ["FIntDebugTextureManagerSkipMips"] = "8",
        }
    }
}

local ACTIVE_QUALITY = nil

local function normalizeFFlagName(name)
    name = tostring(name or "")
    name = name:gsub("^DFInt", ""):gsub("^DFFlag", ""):gsub("^FFFlag", "")
        :gsub("^FFlag", ""):gsub("^FInt", ""):gsub("^FString", ""):gsub("^FLog", "")
    return name
end

local function restoreQualityProfile(profile)
    if not profile or not profile.on then return end
    if type(setfflag) == "function" and profile.captured then
        for name, old in pairs(profile.old) do pcall(setfflag, name, old) end
    end
    profile.on, profile.captured, profile.old = false, false, {}
    if ACTIVE_QUALITY == profile then ACTIVE_QUALITY = nil end
end

local function applyQualityProfile(profile)
    if not profile or closed then return false, 0, "closed" end
    if type(setfflag) ~= "function" or type(getfflag) ~= "function" then
        return false, 0, "unsupported"
    end
    if ACTIVE_QUALITY and ACTIVE_QUALITY ~= profile then restoreQualityProfile(ACTIVE_QUALITY) end
    profile.old, profile.captured = {}, false
    local changed, index = 0, 0
    for name, value in pairs(profile.flags) do
        index = index + 1
        if index % 15 == 0 then task.wait() end
        local realName = normalizeFFlagName(name)
        local okCurrent, current = pcall(getfflag, realName)
        if okCurrent and current ~= nil then
            profile.old[realName] = tostring(current)
            local okSet = pcall(function() setfflag(realName, tostring(value)) end)
            if okSet then changed = changed + 1 end
        end
    end
    profile.captured = next(profile.old) ~= nil
    profile.on = changed > 0
    ACTIVE_QUALITY = profile.on and profile or nil
    return profile.on, changed, profile.on and "done" or "no_valid_flags"
end

local function toggleQualityProfile(profile)
    if not profile then return end

    -- نفس الجودة: إطفاءها.
    if profile.on then
        restoreQualityProfile(profile)
        return
    end

    -- يمنع تبديل الجودة مباشرة؛ لازم تطفي الحالية أولاً.
    if ACTIVE_QUALITY and ACTIVE_QUALITY ~= profile and ACTIVE_QUALITY.on then
        if toast then
            toast("اطفِ الجودة الحالية أولاً")
        end
        return
    end

    applyQualityProfile(profile)
end

    -- ========================= صفحة الجودة =========================
    local pQ = newPage("quality")
    do
        makeToggle(pQ, 0, 0, PAGE_W, 40, function() return "جودة عادية" end,
            function() return QUALITY_PROFILES.normal.on end,
            function() toggleQualityProfile(QUALITY_PROFILES.normal) end)
        makeToggle(pQ, 0, 48, PAGE_W, 40, function() return "جودة كسر فريمات" end,
            function() return QUALITY_PROFILES.message.on end,
            function() toggleQualityProfile(QUALITY_PROFILES.message) end)
        makeToggle(pQ, 0, 96, PAGE_W, 40, function() return "جودة كسر فريمات" end,
            function() return QUALITY_PROFILES.zeneg.on end,
            function() toggleQualityProfile(QUALITY_PROFILES.zeneg) end)
    end

    -- ========================= صفحة الإعدادات =========================
    local pX = newPage("misc")
    do
        makeToggle(pX, 0, 48, PAGE_W, 40, function() return T("قفل الواجهة (ما تتحرك)", "Lock panel (no drag)") end,
            function() return locked end, function() locked = not locked; renderLock() end)
        makeSlider(pX, 0, 96, PAGE_W, T("حجم الواجهة", "UI SIZE"), 0.5, 1.2, 0.05, function() return uiScale.Scale end,
            function(v) uiScale.Scale = v end, function(v) return string.format("%d%%", math.floor(v * 100 + 0.5)) end)
    end


    -- ========================= صفحة الميزات (Headless / Korblox / Wall Hop) =========================
    local pE = newPage("extra")
    do
        local X = {headless = HEADLESS, korblox = KORBLOX, wall = false, kbOrig = nil}

        local function charOf() return lp.Character end

        -- Headless: يخفي الراس (يظهر لك فقط)
        local savedHead = {}
        local function headlessStep()
            local c = charOf()
            local hd = c and c:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if not hd then return end
            local list = {hd}
            for _, d in ipairs(hd:GetChildren()) do if d:IsA("Decal") then table.insert(list, d) end end
            for _, o in ipairs(list) do
                if savedHead[o] == nil then savedHead[o] = o.Transparency end
                o.Transparency = 1
                if o:IsA("BasePart") then o.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1 end
            end
        end
        local function headlessRestore()
            for o, t in pairs(savedHead) do
                pcall(function()
                    if o.Parent then
                        o.Transparency = t
                        if o:IsA("BasePart") then o.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0 end
                    end
                end)
            end
            savedHead = {}
        end
        local hlAcc = 0
        table.insert(conns, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
            if closed or not X.headless then return end
            hlAcc = hlAcc + dt
            if hlAcc < 0.1 then return end
            hlAcc = 0
            pcall(headlessStep)
        end))

        -- Korblox: الرجل اليمين تصير كوربلوكس (تظهر لك فقط)
        local function korbloxSet(on)
            local c = charOf()
            local hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not hum then return end
            pcall(function()
                if on then
                    local d = hum:GetAppliedDescription()
                    if not X.kbOrig then X.kbOrig = d:Clone() end
                    d.RightLeg = KORBLOX_ID
                    hum:ApplyDescription(d)
                elseif X.kbOrig then
                    hum:ApplyDescription(X.kbOrig)
                    X.kbOrig = nil
                end
            end)
        end

        -- Wall Hop: تنط من الجدار وانت بالهوا (كل ضغطة نط تطلعك فوق الجدار)
        local lastHop = 0
        local rp = RaycastParams.new()
        rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        table.insert(conns, UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
            if closed or not X.wall then return end
            local c = charOf()
            local hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not (hum and hrp and hum.Health > 0) then return end
            if hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then return end      -- لازم تكون بالهوا
            if os.clock() - lastHop < 0.18 then return end
            rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = {c}
            local best, bestD = nil, 3.2
            local cf = hrp.CFrame
            for _, dir in ipairs({cf.LookVector, -cf.LookVector, cf.RightVector, -cf.RightVector}) do
                local hit = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, dir * 3.2, rp)
                if hit and math.abs(hit.Normal.Y) < 0.35 and hit.Distance < bestD then
                    best, bestD = hit, hit.Distance
                end
            end
            if not best then return end
            lastHop = os.clock()
            local n = best.Normal
            local jp = hum.UseJumpPower and hum.JumpPower or math.sqrt(2 * workspace.Gravity * hum.JumpHeight)
            -- فوق + شوي ناحية الجدار عشان تظل ملتصق وتكمل نط
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(-n.X * 4, math.max(jp, 50), -n.Z * 4)
        end))

        local function applyAll()
            if X.headless then pcall(headlessStep) end
            if X.korblox then korbloxSet(true) end
        end
        table.insert(conns, lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            X.kbOrig = nil; savedHead = {}
            task.delay(1.5, function() if not closed then applyAll() end end)
        end))
        task.delay(2, function() if not closed then applyAll() end end)

        makeToggle(pE, 0, 0, PAGE_W, 44, function() return "Headless" end,
            function() return X.headless end, function()
                X.headless = not X.headless
                HEADLESS = X.headless
                if not X.headless then headlessRestore() end
            end)
        mkLabel(pE, 4, 46, PAGE_W - 8, 20, T("إخفاء الراس (يظهر لك فقط)", "Hide your head (only you see it)"), 12, C.sub)
        makeToggle(pE, 0, 74, PAGE_W, 44, function() return "Korblox" end,
            function() return X.korblox end, function()
                X.korblox = not X.korblox
                KORBLOX = X.korblox
                korbloxSet(X.korblox)
            end)
        mkLabel(pE, 4, 120, PAGE_W - 8, 20, T("الرجل اليمين تصير Korblox (تظهر لك فقط)", "Right leg becomes Korblox (only you see it)"), 12, C.sub)
        makeToggle(pE, 0, 148, PAGE_W, 44, function() return "WALL HOP" end,
            function() return X.wall end, function() X.wall = not X.wall end)
        mkLabel(pE, 4, 194, PAGE_W - 8, 28, T(
            "قرّب من جدار وانت بالهوا واضغط نط بسرعة.",
            "Get close to a wall while in the air and tap Jump."), 11, C.sub)
    end

    -- ========================= Assist نط =========================
    -- نفس فلترة واستهداف الـAssist الأساسي؛ الإضافة الوحيدة: إذا بدأ الخصم نطة فعلية، تنط شخصيتك معه.
    GATE.assistJump = false
    GATE.assistJumpLast = 0
    GATE.assistJumpTarget = nil
    GATE.assistJumpWasJumping = false

    table.insert(conns, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if closed or not GATE.assistJump then return end

        local character = lp.Character
        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local rootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local heldTool = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
        local backpackTool = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack") and lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"):FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
        if not humanoid or not rootPart or humanoid.Health <= 0 or not (heldTool or backpackTool) then
            GATE.assistJumpTarget = nil
            setTargetText(T("الهدف: بانتظار أداة", "Target: Waiting for Tool"))
            return
        end

        -- نفس قاعدة الـAssist العادي: بدون أداة عندك ما يبحث عن أي خصم.
        refreshGreen()
        local bestRoot, bestDist, bestName = nil, assistRange, ""
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            local pc = p.Character
            local pr = pc and pc:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local th = pc and pc:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if p ~= lp and pr and th and th.Health > 0
                and not isTeammate(p) and not greenCache[p]
                and isInMatch(p) and inArena(p) then
                local d = (rootPart.Position - pr.Position).Magnitude
                if d < bestDist then
                    bestDist = d
                    bestRoot = pr
                    bestName = p.DisplayName or p.Name
                end
            end
        end

        if not bestRoot or not bestRoot.Parent then
            GATE.assistJumpTarget = nil
            return
        end

        GATE.assistJumpTarget = bestRoot
        setTargetText(T("الهدف: ", "Target: ") .. bestName)

        -- حركة الـAssist تبقى كما هي. الإضافة الوحيدة هنا: يقفز عندما يبدأ الخصم قفزة فعلية.
        humanoid:MoveTo(bestRoot.Position)

        local targetHum = bestRoot.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if targetHum then
            local state = targetHum:GetState()
            -- Jump أو حالة Jumping = بداية نطة الخصم.
            local targetJumping = targetHum.Jump == true or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping

            if targetJumping and not GATE.assistJumpWasJumping then
                -- نطة واحدة فقط مع بداية نطة الخصم.
                humanoid.Jump = true
                humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
            end

            -- يمنع تكرار النطة كل Heartbeat؛ ينتظر نطة الخصم التالية.
            GATE.assistJumpWasJumping = targetJumping
        else
            GATE.assistJumpWasJumping = false
        end
    end))

    makeToggle(pE, 0, 224, PAGE_W, 40, function() return "اسيست نط" end,
        function() return GATE.assistJump end, function()
            GATE.assistJump = not GATE.assistJump
            if not GATE.assistJump then
                GATE.assistJumpTarget = nil
                GATE.assistJumpWasJumping = false
            end
        end)

    -- ========================= صفحة المعلومات =========================
    local pI = newPage("info")
    do
        local Stats = game:GetService("Stats")
        local sc = new("ScrollingFrame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
            ScrollBarThickness = 4, ScrollBarImageColor3 = ACC, CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
            AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ZIndex = 33}, pI)
        new("UIListLayout", {Padding = UDim.new(0, 6), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder}, sc)

        local fpsN, fpsShow = 0, 0
        table.insert(conns, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function() fpsN = fpsN + 1 end))
        task.spawn(function()
            while not closed do
                task.wait(1)
                fpsShow, fpsN = fpsN, 0
            end
        end)

        local function stat(words)
            local ls = lp:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
            if not ls then return "-" end
            for _, v in ipairs(ls:GetChildren()) do
                local n = string.lower(v.Name)
                for _, w in ipairs(words) do
                    if string.find(n, w, 1, true) and pcall(function() return v.Value end) then return tostring(v.Value) end
                end
            end
            return "-"
        end
        local function ping()
            local ok, v = pcall(function() return Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue() end)
            return ok and tostring(math.floor(v)) .. " ms" or "-"
        end
        local function exec()
            local ok, n = pcall(function() return identifyexecutor and identifyexecutor() end)
            return (ok and n) and tostring(n) or "Unknown"
        end

        local ROWS = {
            {"👤", T("اسمك", "Name"),            function() return lp.DisplayName end},
            {"🏷", T("يوزرك", "Username"),        function() return lp.Name end},
            {"🆔", T("الآي دي", "ID"),            function() return tostring(lp.UserId) end},
            {"📅", T("عمر الحساب", "Account age"), function() return tostring(lp.AccountAge) .. T(" يوم", " days") end},
            {"⚡", T("الهاك", "Executor"),         exec},
            {"🏆", T("فوزاتك", "Wins"),            function() return stat({"win", "فوز"}) end},
            {"⚔", T("قتلاتك", "Kills"),            function() return stat({"kill", "قتل", "elim"}) end},
            {"👥", T("اللاعبين", "Players"),       function() return tostring(#Players:GetPlayers()) end},
            {"📊", T("فريماتك", "FPS"),            function() return tostring(fpsShow) end},
            {"📡", T("البينق", "Ping"),            ping},
        }
        local labels = {}
        for k, r in ipairs(ROWS) do
            local b = new("TextButton", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 38), BackgroundColor3 = C.off, Text = "",
                AutoButtonColor = false, LayoutOrder = k, ZIndex = 34}, sc)
            corner(b, 10); stroke(b, ACC, 1, 0.7)
            local t = new("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, 0),
                Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = C.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                Text = "", ZIndex = 35}, b)
            labels[k] = t
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()    -- ضغطة = نسخ القيمة
                pcall(function()
                    local v = string.match(t.Text, ": (.*)$") or t.Text
                    if setclipboard then setclipboard(v) end
                end)
            end)
        end
        local function refresh()
            for k, r in ipairs(ROWS) do
                local ok, v = pcall(r[3])
                labels[k].Text = r[1] .. " " .. r[2] .. ": " .. (ok and tostring(v) or "-")
            end
        end
        refresh()
        task.spawn(function()
            while not closed do
                task.wait(0.5)
                if pI.Visible then refresh() end
            end
        end)
    end

    -- ===== سحب الواجهة من الهيدر + ضغط مطوّل للحذف + السلايدرز =====
    local dragging, moved, dragStart, startPos, pressId = false, false, nil, nil, 0
    dragArea.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
        if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            dragging, moved, dragStart, startPos = true, false, i.Position, frame.Position
            pressId = pressId + 1
            local myId = pressId
            task.delay(1.6, function()
                if dragging and pressId == myId and not moved and not closed then end
            end)
        end
    end)
    table.insert(conns, UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
        if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
            if sliding then
                sliding.setFromX(i.Position.X)
            elseif dragging then
                local d = i.Position - dragStart
                if d.Magnitude > 8 then moved = true end
                if moved and not locked then
                    frame.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
                end
            end
        end
    end))
    table.insert(conns, UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
        if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            sliding = nil
            dragging = false
        end
    end))

    showTab("assist")
end
